# Cosmos fork contract

Source paths are relative to the repository root. Declaration tables identify the interfaces in the source files; runtime behavior is defined by their implementations.

## bank-gateway

| Requirement | Behavior |
| --- | --- |
| <code>req.15002</code> | The regenerated sdk/x/bank/types/query.pb.gw.go registers pattern_Query_SupplyOf_1 for GET /cosmos/bank/v1beta1/supply/by_denom beside pattern_Query_SupplyOf_0 (sdk/x/bank/types/query.pb.gw.go) and fills denom from the query string. |
| <code>req.15002</code> | The regenerated sdk/x/bank/types/query.pb.gw.go registers pattern_Query_DenomMetadata_1 for GET /cosmos/bank/v1beta1/denoms_metadata_by_query_string beside pattern_Query_DenomMetadata_0 (sdk/x/bank/types/query.pb.gw.go). |

## baseapp-commit-hooks

| Interface | Kind | Source | Declaration |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| <code>interface.15.go-baseapp-set-deliver-state-to-commit-j56</code> | <code>go_exported</code> | <code>sdk/baseapp/abci.go</code> | <code>func (app *BaseApp) SetDeliverStateToCommit()</code> |
| <code>interface.15.go-baseapp-set-process-proposal-state-to-commit-j56</code> | <code>go_exported</code> | <code>sdk/baseapp/abci.go</code> | <code>func (app *BaseApp) SetProcessProposalStateToCommit()</code> |
| <code>interface.15.go-baseapp-set-sealed-state-to-commit-j56</code> | <code>go_exported</code> | <code>sdk/baseapp/abci.go</code> | <code>func (app *BaseApp) SetSealedStateToCommit(ctx sdk.Context, ms sdk.CacheMultiStore) error</code> |

| Field | Rule |
|---|---|
| interface | BaseApp.SetDeliverStateToCommit, SetProcessProposalStateToCommit and SetSealedStateToCommit in sdk/baseapp/abci.go |
| storage | stateToCommit holds the CacheMultiStore written by WriteState (sdk/baseapp/abci.go) on Commit |
| errors | SetSealedStateToCommit returns an error for a nil multistore |
| events | none |
| gas | none |
| invariants | Commit writes exactly one state per height: deliver, process-proposal or sealed |
| pause | none |

| Requirement | Behavior |
| --- | --- |
| <code>req.15003</code> | SetSealedStateToCommit installs state{ms, ctx} as stateToCommit beside SetDeliverStateToCommit (sdk/baseapp/abci.go), so Commit (sdk/baseapp/abci.go) writes the sealed store and reads the header from its context (sdk/baseapp/abci.go). |
| <code>req.15003</code> | SetSealedStateToCommit refuses a nil CacheMultiStore with an error and leaves stateToCommit unchanged, so Commit keeps its nil guard at sdk/baseapp/abci.go. |

## gas-meter

| Requirement | Behavior |
| --- | --- |
| <code>req.15020</code> | infiniteGasMeter.ConsumeGas (sdk/store/types/gas.go) sets consumed to math.MaxUint64 on overflow as basicGasMeter does at sdk/store/types/gas.go, instead of the 0 returned by addUint64Overflow (sdk/store/types/gas.go). |
| <code>req.15020</code> | After saturation the meter never wraps to a small consumed value, so a recovered overflow cannot be followed by cheap execution (sdk/store/types/gas.go). |

## integration-receipts

| Interface | Kind | Source | Declaration |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| <code>interface.15.env-integrations-layerx-receipt-keys-json-j56</code> | <code>env_var</code> | <code>sdk/integrations/express/src/index.ts</code> | <code>LAYERX_RECEIPT_KEYS_JSON</code> |
| <code>interface.15.fmt-integrations-fastapi-authorized-batch-resolver-j56</code> | <code>file_format</code> | <code>sdk/integrations/fastapi/layerx_fastapi/seller.py</code> | <code>AuthorizedBatchResolver = tuple[PxReceiptKeyEntry, ...]</code> |

The first-party integrations verify signed transaction-bound sequencer receipts against configured receipt keys. Payment release checks the requested commitment and payment log; a claimed committed rung requires independently verified block evidence.

| Integration | Receipt and configuration implementation |
| --- | --- |
| Express | [index.ts](../../../sdk/integrations/express/src/index.ts): `receiptKeys`, loaded from `LAYERX_RECEIPT_KEYS_JSON`, configures the seller middleware and `ReceiptPayloadAuthority`. |
| Next | [index.ts](../../../sdk/integrations/next/src/index.ts): the same receipt-key configuration is passed to the payment middleware. |
| FastAPI | [config.py](../../../sdk/integrations/fastapi/layerx_fastapi/config.py), [seller.py](../../../sdk/integrations/fastapi/layerx_fastapi/seller.py), [protocol.py](../../../sdk/integrations/fastapi/layerx_fastapi/protocol.py): receipt-key parsing, fulfillment and sequencer receipt verification. |
| Spring | [LayerXDeclaredConfig.java](../../../sdk/integrations/spring/src/main/java/com/sidiora/layerx/spring/LayerXDeclaredConfig.java), [SellerMiddleware.java](../../../sdk/integrations/spring/src/main/java/com/sidiora/layerx/spring/SellerMiddleware.java): receipt keys and transaction-bound payment evidence. |
| Android | [ReceiptGate.java](../../../sdk/integrations/android/src/main/java/com/sidiora/layerx/android/ReceiptGate.java): receipt evidence is checked against the pinned key set and height bounds. |
| iOS | [ReceiptGate.swift](../../../sdk/integrations/ios/Sources/LayerXMobile/ReceiptGate.swift): signed receipt verification under the configured key set. |
| Agents | [daemon-providers.ts](../../../sdk/integrations/agents/src/daemon-providers.ts), [tools.ts](../../../sdk/integrations/agents/src/tools.ts), [services.ts](../../../sdk/integrations/agents/src/services.ts): signed receipt evidence crosses daemon, tool and service boundaries. |

<a id="legacy-telemetry"></a>

## SDK telemetry

SDK components declare OpenTelemetry meters and record counters, gauges and duration histograms in their execution paths. Instrument names and units are defined in the component source files. The separate [telemetry package](../../../sdk/telemetry/metrics.go) configures the application's telemetry sinks and output formats.

| Component | Meter | Instruments | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `sdk/baseapp` | `paxcosmos_baseapp` | `mid_block_duration`, `end_block_duration`, `deliver_tx_duration`, `tx`, `tx_result`, `tx_gas_used`, `tx_gas_wanted`, `commit_duration`, `abci_query_duration`, `process_proposal_duration`, `finalize_block_duration`, `get_tx_priority_hint_duration`, `run_tx_duration`, `run_msgs_duration`, `run_msg_latency` | [metrics.go](../../../sdk/baseapp/metrics.go) |
| `sdk/storev2/rootmulti` | `paxcosmos_storev2_rootmulti` | `sc_commit_latency`, `ss_version`, `historical_abci_query`, `iavl_total_key_bytes`, `iavl_total_value_bytes`, `iavl_total_num_keys`, `state_sync_keys_exported` | [metrics.go](../../../sdk/storev2/rootmulti/metrics.go) |
| `sdk/store/types` | `paxcosmos_store_types` | `gas_exceeded`, `last_evicted_keys_bounded_cache` | [metrics.go](../../../sdk/store/types/metrics.go) |
| `sdk/x/staking` | `paxcosmos_x_staking` | `staking_begin_blocker_duration`, `staking_end_blocker_duration` | [metrics.go](../../../sdk/x/staking/metrics.go) |
| `sdk/x/slashing` | `paxcosmos_x_slashing` | `slashing_begin_blocker_duration` | [metrics.go](../../../sdk/x/slashing/metrics.go) |
| `sdk/x/capability` | `paxcosmos_x_capability` | `capability_begin_blocker_duration` | [metrics.go](../../../sdk/x/capability/metrics.go) |
| `sdk/x/bank` | `paxcosmos_x_bank` | `init_genesis_duration` | [metrics.go](../../../sdk/x/bank/metrics.go) |
| `sdk/x/distribution` | `paxcosmos_x_distribution` | `distribution_begin_blocker_duration` | [metrics.go](../../../sdk/x/distribution/metrics.go) |
| `sdk/x/gov` | `paxcosmos_x_gov` | `gov_end_blocker_duration` | [metrics.go](../../../sdk/x/gov/metrics.go) |
